Let's design robust code with phases:

```
def soft_obj_grad(x, beta, n, topK=0):
    if n == 1: ...
    x_ext = np.empty(n)
    x_ext[:-1]=x
    x_ext[-1]=0.0
    m = x_ext.max()
    e = np.exp(x_ext - m)
    a = e / e.sum()
    c = fast_convolve(a)
    cmax = c.max()
    if topK and topK < len(c):
       idx=np.argpartition(c, -topK)[-topK:]
       cd=c[idx]
       shifted=beta*(cd-cd.max())
       p=np.exp(shifted); p/=p.sum()
       Slog=np.log(np.exp(cd.max() - cmax)*p.sum())? Wait LSE over subset: LSE=cd.max()+logS/beta? But if we exclude far, it's not same as global max; at optimum maybe max in subset. Use subset LSE = cdmax + log(S)/beta. But F may miss if global max not in topK? We choose topK of current c; after update, global max might be outside but line search may not see. Better use all peaks or topK large enough.
```

```
def build_conv_matrix(a):
   first_col = np.zeros(2*n-1); first_col[:n]=a
   first_row = np.zeros(n); first_row[0]=a[0]
   return scipy.linalg.toeplitz(first_col, first_row)
```
Need check toeplitz returns matrix with first col and first row. For cov matrix row k has a_{k-j}. first_col row k for j=0: a_k if k<n else 0. first row row0 j: a_{0-j}=a0 if j=0 else0. Yes.
LP:
```
def lp_direction(a, M, U=10.0):
   n=len(a); F=build_conv_matrix(a)
   c_obj = -np.ones(n)
   res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=F, b_ub=M*(1+1e-9), bounds=[(0,U)]*n, method='highs')
   if res.success:
       g=res.x; S=g.sum(); if S>1e-8: return g/S, S
   return None, 0
```
But building F each iteration O(m*n) in Python maybe using toeplitz C? Good. LP with dense 1535x768 maybe OK.
However HiGhS with bounds list of tuples maybe okay. We can use bounds=(0,U) as `bounds=[(0.0, U)] * n`? That creates same tuple? fine. Or `bounds=slice(0,U)`? linprog accepts array of (min,max) or slice? Use list.
Upper bound U: Since g entries can be >10? If normalized sum 1, any entry >10 impossible (sum 1). But original g_opt before normalization may have entries >10? It will be normalized; if bound too low, direction constrained. Feasible original g = a*S; a entries maybe ≤0.1, S maybe few; entries ≤0.5. U=10 safe. If a has large endpoint 0.1, g original maybe >? LP maximize sum can scale until constraints bind; entries ≤M/max a ~0.001/0.1=0.01? Actually if a endpoint large 0.03, M~0.001, bound 0.02. U=10 safe. Good.

Line search for LP direction:
```
def lp_polish(a0, time_limit, ...):
  a=a0.copy(); M=current max; best...
  while time<limit:
     g,S=lp_direction(a,M)
     if g is None: break
     # maybe add small noise
     candidates t: [0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3]
     best_t = None; best_obj = M
     for t in [0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5]:
         a_t = (1-t)*a + t*g
         a_t = max 0; normalize
         obj=fast_convolve(a_t).max()
         if obj < best_obj - 1e-15: best...
     if no improve: try smaller t (0.001) maybe; else break
     a=best_a; M=best_obj
     if M < best: update
```

Let's design final algorithm more concretely.

Variables:
- n_base = len(parent) if available else 768.
- Deadline = time.monotonic() + budget - 30 (reserve). Use budget_s parameter default 1800.

`quick_score(a)`:
```
def quick_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: return inf
    c=fast_convolve(a/s)
    return 2*len(a)*c.max()
```
But evaluate_sequence returns same for nonnegative finite. For speed, use this and final evaluate_sequence.

`normalize(a)`: a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s>0 return a/s else uniform.

Let's refine BFGS implementation.

```
def softmax_from_x(x, n):
    xext = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    xext[:-1] = x
    xext[-1] = 0.0
    mx = np.max(xext)
    e = np.exp(xext - mx)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not finite:
       return np.ones(n)/n
    return e/s

def next_fft_len(m):
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```
For conv length need Lconv = next_fft_len(2*n). For gradient Lgrad = next_fft_len(3*n) maybe. We can pass Lconv, Lgrad in params to avoid recompute.

`soft_f(x, n, beta, Lconv)`:
```
xext... a
c = fast_convolve_len(a, Lconv) # use Lconv
cmax = c.max()
# if beta inf? use temp? We'll use beta float.
shifted = beta * (c - cmax)
# underflow ok; but if beta*cvery negative, -inf? c-cmax finite ~ -1, beta 1e5 -> exp(-1e5)=0. ok.
S = np.exp(shifted).sum()
lse = cmax + np.log(S)/beta
return n*lse
```
Potential if beta very large and cmax huge? c~1e-3, fine. If beta=1e6, shifted for c=0 -> exp(-978) underflows to0 safe. If c-cmax maybe -1e-15, exp(-1e-9) ~1.

`soft_fg(x,...)` compute f and grad:
```
a = softmax
A = rfft(a, Lgrad) # Lgrad enough for conv and gradient. Use Lgrad for conv too, c = irfft(A*A, Lgrad)[:m]
cmax...
S... p = exp(shifted)/S
# grad_a via correlation:
P = np.zeros(Lgrad); P[:m]=p
APH = rfft(P)  # P real, Pfr
g_half = irfft(APH * np.conj(A), Lgrad)[:n]
# Is g_half = ∑ p_{i+j} a_i. Then grad_a = 2*g_half.
Sx = np.dot(g_half, a)  # since grad_a=2g; S for LSE = dot(grad_a,a)=2 dot(g_half,a)
grad_x = np.empty(n-1)
grad_x[:n-1] = a[:-1] * (2*g_half[:-1] - 2*Sdot)
grad_x *= n
# f = n*lse
return f, grad_x, a, cmax
```
Check Sx: grad_a=2g. Sum_i grad_a_i a_i =2∑g_i a_i =2 LSE? Since ∑_i ∑_k p_k a_i a_{k-i} = ∑_k p_k c_k = LSE. Yes Sx=2*LSE. grad_x = a*(grad_a - grad_a·a) if no n scaling? For objective LSE, grad_x_j = ∑_i grad_a_i a_i(δ_ij-a_j)=grad_a_j a_j - a_j ∑ grad_a_i a_i. Multiply n. So `n*a*(grad_a - Sgrad)` where Sgrad=∑ grad_a_i a_i. So `n*a*(2*g - 2*Sdot)`. Good.

Need ensure gradient formula with softmax fixed last=0: For x length n-1, a_i for i<n-1 depends on x_i, last fixed. The derivation for unconstrained x_ext with last variable not included: grad wrt x_i = a_i(∑? Derived same for i=0..n-2. Good.

Line search using `soft_f`. But if beta high and F nearly max, line search may accept small decreases. Use Armijo:
```
f0, g, a0 = soft_fg(x)
f_best_actual = actual score
if norm(g) < 1e-10 break
W = 20 history arrays
for iter:
    if need_recompute f/g (after restart) ...
    compute d
    alpha=alpha_init
    for ls in range(40):
       x_trial = x + alpha*d
       # maybe clip x_trial to [-50,50]? But fixed last 0; clipping may break. Use x_trial = np.clip(x_trial, -100, 100) to prevent extremes? But gradients at clip? no. Maybe not.
       f_trial = soft_f(x_trial)
       if f_trial <= f0 + 1e-4*alpha*np.dot(g,d) + 1e-16: accept
       alpha *= 0.5
       if alpha < 1e-8: fail
    if fail:
       # try pure negative gradient with smaller alpha or restart with noise
```

LP min cross:
```
def min_cross_lp(a, M=None):
   n=len(a); F=build_conv_matrix(a)
   # A_ub shape (m, n+1): F, -1
   A_ub = np.column_stack((F, -np.ones(m)))
   b_ub = np.zeros(m)
   A_eq = np.zeros((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,:n]=1
   b_eq=np.array([1.0])
   c_obj = np.concatenate([np.zeros(n), [1.0]])
   res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq, bounds=[(0,None)]*n + [(0,None)], method='highs')
   if success: g=res.x[:n]; C=res.x[n]; normalize g small; return g,C
```
This LP has no upper bounds, but equality sum=1 bounds g entries ≤1; C bounded. Good. Matrix includes column -1; fine.
Feasibility: g=a, C=M feasible. If a has zeros, still bounded by sum.
This should be robust. The optimal C may be slightly less than current M. Since a itself feasible, if LP returns C > M due numerical, set C=M or fail.

Then line search toward g. Since g is in simplex, t∈[0,1]. The objective function in max conv may not be convex, but we can evaluate. If we find t with bound ≤ M - δ, actual improves guaranteed. We can solve or search. This can take larger t if g good. Use this to select t candidates: e.g., t from 0 to1. We can line search actual.

LP polish function:
```
def lp_polish(a0, time_limit, verbose=False, label="", max_iter=200, no_improve_limit=30):
    a = normalize(a0)
    M = fast_convolve(a).max()
    best = (2*n*M)
    best_a = a.copy()
    t0=time...
    no=0
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time > limit: break
       g, C = min_cross_lp(a)
       if g is None: break
       # ensure sum=1, nonneg
       g = np.maximum(g,0); g /= g.sum()
       # compute actual D, Ccross maybe from arrays
       Ccross = min_cross_actual(a,g) # maybe use LP C, but recompute to be safe
       D = fast_convolve(g).max()
       # Candidate t values
       t_vals = np.array([0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7,1.0])
       # also solve upper bound for t: quadratic in t <= current M? let current M_target
       # We can generate t where bound = M*(1-eps)
       #solve q(t)=a t^2 + b t + c with a=M-D, b=2(C-M), c=0? q=(1-t)^2 M +2t(1-t)C +t^2D = M + t[2(C-M)+t(D-2C+M)]
       # We want <=M - delta. Find root? Simpler include.
       best_t=None; best_obj=M
       for t in t_vals:
          at = (1-t)*a + t*g; at=max0; at/=sum
          obj=fast_convolve(at).max()
          if obj < best_obj - 1e-15:
             best_obj=obj; best_t=t; best_at=at
       if best_t is None:
          # try smaller t maybe 0.001, 0.005
          for t in [0.001,0.005]: ...
          if still none: break/no_improve++
       a=best_at; M=best_obj
       if score < best - tol: best...
       else no_improve++
       if no_improve>no_limit: maybe perturb or break
    return best_a
```

Implement `fast_convolve(a, L=None)`:
```
def fast_convolve(a, L=None):
   m=len(a); size=2*m-1
   if L is None: L=1 << (size-1).bit_length()
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
   return np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)[:size]
```
For cross conv `fast_convolve_cross(a,b,L)`: c=irfft(rfft(a,L)*rfft(b,L),L)[:2n-1]? Since both length n.

```
def bfgs_optimize(a0, beta, time_limit, max_iter=500, verbose=False, label=""):
    n=len(a0)
    a_start=np.maximum(a0,1e-12)/sum
    if n==1: return a_start
    x=np.log(a_start[:-1]/a_start[-1])
    x=np.clip(x,-700,700)
    Lconv=next_fft_len(2*n-1)
    Lgrad=next_fft_len(3*n)
    f, g, a, cmax = soft_fg(x,beta,n,Lconv,Lgrad)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_cmax=cmax
    # history
    m=20; Ws=[]; Wg=[]; Wr=[]
    alpha=0.1
    no_improve=0
    t0=time...
    it=0
    while time < limit:
       it++
       # stop if grad norm small
       gnorm=np.linalg.norm(g)
       if gnorm < 1e-10: break
       # direction
       if not Ws:
          d=-g
       else:
          alpha_l=0.0
          s_last=Ws[-1]; g_last=Wg[-1]; rho=1/dot(s_last,g_last)
          q = g - rho*dot(rho,g_last)? Need standard two-loop.
```
Standard L-BFGS:
```
s = x - x_prev
y = g - g_prev
if y_T s > 0: store s,y,1/(yTs)
to compute direction d:
    q = g.copy()
    for i reversed(m-1..0):
        alpha_i = Wr[i] * dot(Ws[i], q)
        q = q - alpha_i * Wg[i]
    r = q * gamma  # gamma = dot(s_last,y_last)/dot(y_last,y_last)
    for i 0..m-1:
        beta_i = Wr[i] * dot(Wg[i], r)
        r = r + (alpha_i - beta_i)*Ws[i]
    d = -r
```
Need store arrays Ws,Wy,Wr. If store less than m. `gamma` from last pair if any; if not, gamma=1. Use `gamma = max(1e-8, dot(s,y)/dot(y,y))`.

```
def score_normalized(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300: return inf,0
    aa=a/s
    c=fast_convolve(aa)
    m=c.max()
    return 2*len(a)*m, m, aa
```

```
def build_conv_matrix(a):
    n=len(a); m=2*n-1
    first_col=np.zeros(m); first_col[:n]=a
    first_row=np.zeros(n); first_row[0]=a[0]
    return scipy.linalg.toeplitz(first_col, first_row)
```
But if a[0] is 0, first row all zeros? Then row0 has only? For row0, F[0,0]=a0=0, row all zeros? Convolution first coefficient a0*a? If a0=0, row0 all zeros indeed? Wait (a*g)_0 = a0*g0. Min cross LP:
```
def min_cross_lp(a, time_budget=None):
   if not HAVE_SCIPY: return None,None
   n=len(a); m=2*n-1
   F=build_conv_matrix(a)
   A_ub = np.empty((m,n+1)); A_ub[:,:n]=F; A_ub[:,n]=-1
   b_ub=np.zeros(m)
   A_eq=np.zeros((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,:n]=1
   c=np.zeros(n+1); c[n]=1
   bounds=[(0.0, None)]*n + [(0.0, None)]
   res=linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=np.array([1.0]), bounds=bounds, method='highs')
   if res.status !=0: return None,None
   g=res.x[:n]; C=res.x[n]
   g=np.maximum(g,0)
   s=g.sum()
   if s<=0: return None,None
   g/=s
   return g, float(C)
```
Could optimize memory by not storing F separately: Create A_ub directly using arrays? `np.column_stack` maybe copy. We can do:


```
def trim_ends(a, score, max_steps=100):
   for _ in range(max_steps):
      best_pair=(0,score,a)
      for end in ['last','first']:
         if len(a)<16: break
         b=a[:-1] or a[1:]
         b=np.maximum(b,0); s=b.sum()
         if s<=0: continue
         b/=s; sc,_,_=quick_score(b)
         if sc+1e-14<score: best...
      if best_pair: a,b...; score=... else break
   return a,score
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    import time? already
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, budget_s - 30.0)
    best_a = None; best_score=inf
    parent=None
    try parent from global
    if parent: normalize, score; update best
    n_parent=len(parent) if parent else 768
    # initial scans
    n_list = []
    for n in [n_parent, 768, 1024, int(n_parent*1.25), int(n_parent*1.5), int(n_parent*2)]:
         if 16<=n<=2048 and n not in n_list: add
    # limit initial scan time 30s
    init_deadline=time+30
    init_best_by_n={}
    for n in n_list:
        a,s = best_candidate_for_n(n,parent, max_eval=25 if n>1024 else 40)
        store; update global best if better
        if time>init_deadlen: break
    # choose primary_initial: if global best a length maybe; but for LP need n<=1024; choose among candidates with n<=1024 best, else best
    lp_candidate = choose best score among stored with n<=1024 (if any), else global best if n<=1024 else stored parent
    # Phase LP
    if lp_candidate is not None and n<=LP_MAX_N:
       t_allocate = min(400, remaining*0.35)
       best_a, best_score = lp_polish(lp_candidate, time_limit=t_allocate, verbose=True, label='LP1')
    # Phase BFGS on best
    if remaining > 100:
       t_b1 = min(250, remaining*0.3)
       best_a, best_score = bfgs_optimize(best_a, beta=50000, time_limit=t_b1,...)
    # Phase LP2 if time and n<=LP_MAX_N
       t_lp2=min(300,remaining*0.3)
    # BFGS high beta
       t_b2=min(200,remaining*0.3) beta 150000
    # Maybe run BFGS on secondary candidate different n if time >150
    for other init in init_best_by_n not same as best, if score within 1e-5 and length<=2048: run bfgs 120s
    # Trim ends and final eval
    best_a,_ = trim_ends(best_a,best_score)
    final_score = evaluate_sequence(best_a.tolist())
    if not finite: fallback
    return [float(x) for x in best_a]
```

Now, implement `bfgs_optimize` carefully. Potential performance issue: `soft_fg` uses P_hat each eval; P length Lgrad zeros every time allocation. Could optimize by reusing arrays? Top-level no closures but can allocate. Fine.
`soft_fg` code:
```
def soft_fg(x, beta, n, Lconv, Lgrad):
    xext = np.empty(n)
    xext[:-1] = x
    xext[-1] = 0.0
    mx = np.max(xext)
    e = np.exp(xext - mx)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        a = np.ones(n)/n
    else:
        a = e / s
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, Lgrad)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A*A, Lgrad)[:2*n-1]
    cmax = np.max(c)
    shifted = beta*(c - cmax)
    # if beta == inf? not
    weights = np.exp(shifted)
    S = weights.sum()
    if S <=0 or not finite:
       weights = np.zeros(2*n-1); weights[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; S=1.0
    p = weights / S
    lse = cmax + np.log(S)/beta
    f = n*lse
    P = np.zeros(Lgrad)
    P[:2*n-1] = p
    PH = np.fft.rfft(P)
    g_half = np.fft.irfft(PH * np.conj(A), Lgrad)[:n]
    g_half = np.real(g_half)
    Sdot = float(np.dot(g_half, a))
    grad_x = np.empty(n-1)
    if n > 1:
       grad_x = n * (2.0 * g_half[:-1] - 2.0 * Sdot) * a[:-1]
    return f, grad_x, a, cmax
```
Check derivative of LSE: F=n*lse, grad = n*a*(2g - 2Sdot). If x length n-1. Good.
But `g_half` from FFT might have small imaginary? irfft real. Values positive/zero. Sdot ≈ lse. LSE = ∑ p_k c_k. Good.
Potential numerical: If beta very large, `np.log(S)/beta` small; if S ~ active count, lse accurate. If beta=0? not.

`soft_f` faster:
```
def soft_f(x,beta,n,Lconv):
  a=softmax; A=rfft(a,Lconv); c=irfft(A*A,Lconv)[:m]
  cmax=...
  shifted=beta*(c-cmax)
  S=exp(shifted).sum()
  if S<=0: return n*cmax
  return n*(cmax+np.log(S)/beta)
```
Could if `shifted.min() < -745`, exp underflow ok. If `shifted` has NaN due c NaN? guard.

```
if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return 1e300
```

Pseudo:
```
def bfgs_optimize(a0, beta, time_limit, verbose=False, label=""):
    a0=np.asarray(a0,float)
    n=len(a0)
    if n<=1: return a0.copy(), score
    a_start=np.maximum(a0,1e-12); a_start/=a_start.sum()
    x=np.log(a_start[:-1]/a_start[-1]); x=np.clip(x,-700,700)
    Lconv=next_pow2(2*n-1); Lgrad=next_pow2(3*n)
    f,g,a,cmax=soft_fg(...)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_cmax=cmax; best_score=2*n*best_cmax
    t0=time.monotonic()
    m=20; S_hist=[]; Y_hist=[]; R_hist=[]
    alpha=0.1; no_improve=0; it=0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(12345)
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
       it+=1
       if not finite: restart
       gnorm=np.linalg.norm(g)
       if gnorm < 1e-9: break
       # direction
       if not S_hist: d=-g
       else: ...
       gd=np.dot(g,d)
       if gd > -1e-14:
           d=-g; gd=-gnorm*gnorm
           if gd > -1e-14: break
       accepted=False
       # line search
       alpha_ls=alpha
       for _ in range(40):
          x_trial=x+alpha_ls*d
          if not finite: alpha_ls*=0.5; continue
          f_trial=soft_f(...)
          if not finite: alpha_ls*=0.5; continue
          if f_trial <= f + 1e-4*alpha_ls*gd:
             accepted=True; break
          alpha_ls*=0.5
          if alpha_ls<1e-8: break
       if not accepted:
          # try direct small step negative gradient
          d=-g; gd=-gnorm*gnorm
          for _ in range(20):
             x_trial=x+alpha_ls*d
             f_trial=soft_f(...)
             if f_trial <= f*(1+1e-7) + 1e-4*alpha_ls*gd:
                accepted=True; break
             alpha_ls*=0.5
       if not accepted:
          # restart
          x = x + rng.standard_normal(n-1)*0.05
          x = np.clip(x,-700,700)
          f,g,a,cmax=soft_fg(...)
          S_hist=[]...; no_improve+=1; if no_improve>50 break; continue
       # evaluate full at accepted x_trial (we have x_trial)
       f_full,g_trial,a_trial,cmax_trial=soft_fg(x_trial,...)
       # accept if F not much worse or actual cmax improves
       if f_full <= f*(1+1e-7) + 1e-14 or cmax_trial < best_cmax - 1e-13:
           # update history
           s_new=x_trial-x; y_new=g_trial-g; ys=dot(s_new,y_new)
           if ys > 1e-10:
              S_hist.append(s_new); Y_hist.append(y_new); R_hist.append(1/ys)
              if len> m: pop(0)
           x=x_trial; f=f_full; g=g_trial; a=a_trial
           if cmax_trial < best_cmax - 1e-13:
              best_cmax=cmax_trial; best_a=a_trial.copy(); best_score=2*n*best_cmax; no_improve=0
              if verbose and it%20==0: print(...)
           else:
              no_improve+=1
           # adapt alpha: if accepted with full ls, could increase? keep
           if no_improve>30:
              # random restart small
              x=x+rng.standard_normal(n-1)*0.02; clip; reset hist; f,g,a,cmax=soft_fg(...)
              no_improve=0 if cmax<... else no_improve? maybe
    return best_a, best_score
```

Pseudo:
```
def generate_starters(n, parent=None, limit=40):
    out=[]
    # uniform
    a=np.ones(n,float); a/=a.sum(); out.append(a)
    # powers left
    idx=np.arange(n)+1.0
    jdx=n-idx
    for p in [0.0,0.2,0.4,0.45,0.5,0.6]:
       a=idx**(-p); ...
       # if p=0 uniform duplicate
    # additive mix
    for p in [0.4,0.45,0.5]:
      left=idx**(-p)
      for q in [0.0,0.2,0.4,0.5]:
         right=jdx**(-q)
         for gamma in [0.0,0.25,0.5,1.0]:
             a=left+gamma*right; normalize; append
    # product
    for p in [0.2,0.4,0.5]:
      for q in [0.0,0.2,0.4]:
         a=idx**(-p)*jdx**(-q); append
    # gaussians
    x=np.arange(n)/n
    for mu in [0.3,0.5,0.7]:
      for sig in [0.1,0.2]:
         a=np.exp(-((x-mu)/sig)**2); append
    if parent is not None:
       a=interpolate_array(parent,n); append
    # Maybe reverse versions? Since reversing sequence yields same score? A reversed: A -> z^{n-1}A, A^2 coefficients reversed, max same. So no need.
    # limit
    if len(out)>limit: out=out[:limit]
    return out
```
Duplicates okay. We can quickly score each. For speed, limit 30 for large n.

`interpolate_array(a, n)`:
```
old=len(a); old_x=np.linspace(0,1,old); new_x=np.linspace(0,1,n); b=np.interp(new_x, old_x, a); b=np.maximum(b,0); s=b.sum(); if s>0: b/=s else uniform.
```

Potential bug: For convolution matrix, row k and column j coefficient a_{k-j}. first_col for j=0: row k = a_k if k<n else 0. first_row for k=0: column j = a_{-j} if j=0 => a0 else 0. `toeplitz(first_col, first_row)` will produce row k (k>0) col j = first_row[j-k]? For Toeplitz, A[i,j]= first_col[i] if j=0; if i=0 first_row[j]; for i,j>0 depends on i-j? It generates constant diagonals. For our matrix, A[i,j] = a_{i-j} if 0≤i-j<n else 0. This is Toeplitz with first column [a0,a1,...,a_{n-1},0...] and first row [a0,0,0,...] indeed. For i=1,j=1 => first row? Toeplitz A[1,1]=A[0,0]=a0, formula i-j=0 ->a0. Good. For i=2,j=1 => i-j=1 -> a1; Toeplitz diagonal from first_col[1]. Good. For i=2,j=3 => i-j=-1 -> 0; first row? good.

```
def fast_convolve_cross(a,b):
```

```
    a=normalize_positive(a0); n=len(a)
    c=fast_convolve(a); M=float(c.max()); best_a=a.copy(); best_M=M; best_score=2*n*M
    t0=time.monotonic(); no_improve=0; it=0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(987)
    perturb=None
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:

       if n > LP_MAX_N? This function shouldn't called otherwise.

       if np.max(np.abs(g-a)) < 1e-13:
```

Detailed code:

```
import numpy as np
import time
try:
   import scipy.optimize as _scipy_opt
   import scipy.linalg as _scipy_linalg
   HAVE_SCIPY=True
except Exception:
   HAVE_SCIPY=False

_next_pow2_cache = {}? no need.
def next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```

`normalize_positive`:
```
def normalize_positive(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s > 0 and np.isfinite(s): return a/s
    return np.ones_like(a)/len(a)
```

`fast_convolve` as above. Need if a length 1, L=1? `next_pow2(1)`? size=1. `(size-1).bit_length()` for 0 ->0, 1<<0=1. Good.

`fast_convolve_cross(a,b,L=None)` similar. If both same length n, size=2*n-1. Use rfft length L.

`quick_score(a)` returns (score, cmax, normalized_a):
```
def quick_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s): return inf,0,None
    aa=a/s
    c=fast_convolve(aa)
    m=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    return float(2*len(aa)*m), m, aa
```

`interpolate_array`.

`build_conv_matrix`:
```
def build_conv_matrix(a):
    n=len(a); m=2*n-1
    first_col=np.zeros(m,float); first_col[:n]=a
    first_row=np.zeros(n,float); first_row[0]=a[0]
    return _scipy_linalg.toeplitz(first_col, first_row)
```

`min_cross_lp(a, perturb=None)`:
```
def min_cross_lp(a, perturb=None):
    if not HAVE_SCIPY: return None,None
    n=len(a); m=2*n-1
    F=build_conv_matrix(a)
    A_ub=np.empty((m,n+1),float)
    A_ub[:,:n]=F
    A_ub[:,n]=-1.0
    b_ub=np.zeros(m,float)
    A_eq=np.zeros((1,n+1),float); A_eq[0,:n]=1.0
    b_eq=np.array([1.0])
    c_obj=np.zeros(n+1,float); c_obj[n]=1.0
    if perturb is not None:
       # ensure finite, small
       p=np.asarray(perturb,float)
       if p.shape==(n,):
          c_obj[:n] -= 1e-7 * p
    bounds=[(0.0, None) for _ in range(n)] + [(0.0, None)]
    # run
    try:
       res=_scipy_opt.linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
    except Exception:
       try: res=_scipy_opt.linprog(..., options={'presolve': True})
       except Exception: return None,None
    if res.status !=0 or not hasattr(res,'x'): return None,None
    g=res.x[:n].astype(float)
    C=float(res.x[n])
    g=np.maximum(g,0.0)
    s=g.sum()
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s): return None,None
    g/=s
    return g,C
```
The list comprehension `[ (0.0,None) for _ in range(n)]` uses not lambda, fine. It creates many tuples; okay. For n=1024, fine.
Potential if `perturb` has large values, scale 1e-7. The objective C is ~1e-3, perturbation sum g dot p ~ up to 1e-7, small. Good.

`lp_polish` as above. Need if n > maybe 2000, LP matrix too large; caller checks. Use `LP_MAX_N = 1024` constant top-level? Define `LP_MAX_N = 1024`. If parent n=768. Maybe set 1152.

`lp_polish` detailed code with seen set:
```
def lp_polish(a0, time_limit, verbose=False, label=""):
    a=normalize_positive(a0); n=len(a)
    c=fast_convolve(a); M=float(c.max()); best_a=a.copy(); best_M=M; best_score=2.0*n*M
    t0=time.monotonic(); no_improve=0; it=0; rng=np.random.default_rng(20240101)
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
       it+=1
       if it > 5000: break
       perturb = rng.standard_normal(n) if it % 20 == 0 else None
       g, C_lp = min_cross_lp(a, perturb=perturb)
       if g is None: break
       if float(np.max(np.abs(g-a))) < 1e-13:
           no_improve +=1
           if no_improve > 5: break
           continue
       Ccross = float(fast_convolve_cross(a,g).max())
       D = float(fast_convolve(g).max())
       # if Ccross >= M + 1e-10 and t=1 not better? still try
       vals=[]; seen=set()
       base=[0.001,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.75,1.0]
       if Ccross < M:
          den = M - 2*Ccross + D
          if den > 1e-14:
             tstar=(M-Ccross)/den
             for fac in (0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0,1.25,1.5):
                t=tstar*fac
                if 0.0 < t < 1.0: base.append(t)
       for t in base:
           key=round(float(t),6)
           if key not in seen:
              seen.add(key); vals.append(float(t))
       best_at=None; best_M_iter=M; best_t=None
       for t in vals:
           at=(1.0-t)*a + t*g
           at=np.maximum(at,0.0)
           st=at.sum()
           if st <= 0 or not np.isfinite(st): continue
           at/=st
           obj=float(fast_convolve(at).max())
           if obj < best_M_iter - 1e-14:
              best_M_iter=obj; best_at=at; best_t=t
       if best_at is None:
           no_improve +=1
           if no_improve > 12:
              # try a random jitter of a and continue? Maybe break
              break
           continue
       a=best_at; M=best_M_iter
       score=2.0*n*M
       if score < best_score - 1e-10:
           best_score=score; best_a=a.copy(); no_improve=0
           if verbose and it % 3 == 0: print(f"  {label} lp iter {it}: score={best_score:.8f} t={best_t:.3f} C={Ccross:.8e}")
       else:
           no_improve +=1
       if no_improve > 40:
           break
    return best_a, best_score
```
Note: We update `a` even if score not less than best? It improved current M but maybe not best? Actually if `best_M_iter < M` previous, then score < current 2nM. If not global best, still progress. If global best not updated but score same? no_improve.

If Ccross from LP is >M due perturb, line search may not improve; no_improve. Perturb every 20 iterations might cause; okay.

Now, BFGS functions. Let's implement `soft_f`, `soft_fg` with `fix_last` only. Use local `m=2*n-1` repeatedly? Pass `m`? Could compute. To reduce overhead, pass `m` and `Lconv`,`Lgrad`.

`soft_f(x, beta, n, Lconv, m)`:
```
def soft_f(x, beta, n, Lconv, m):
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return 1e300
    xext=np.empty(n,float); xext[:-1]=x; xext[-1]=0.0
    mx=np.max(xext)
    if not np.isfinite(mx): return 1e300
    e=np.exp(xext-mx)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       a=np.ones(n)/n
    else:
       a=e/s
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, Lconv)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, Lconv)[:m]
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax): return 1e300
    shifted=beta*(c-cmax)
    # To avoid overflow? c-cmax <=0, exp <=1.
    S=float(np.exp(shifted).sum())
    if S<=0 or not np.isfinite(S):
       idx=int(np.argmax(c)); p=np.zeros(m); p[idx]=1; S=1
    else:
       lse=cmax+np.log(S)/beta
    f=n*lse
    if not np.isfinite(f): return 1e300
    return f
```
If beta very large, `shifted` may be -inf, exp 0; max 0=>1. S≥1.

`soft_fg` similarly but needs p for gradient. It computes A with Lgrad (which is >=Lconv). c length m. Then `shifted`, weights, S. `p=weights/S`. If S problematic, p one-hot. Then f. Then gradient. For `P = np.zeros(Lgrad); P[:m]=p`. `PH = np.fft.rfft(P)`. `g_half = np.fft.irfft(PH * np.conj(A), Lgrad)[:n]`. Need A computed with Lgrad: `A=np.fft.rfft(a,Lgrad)`. Then c from `irfft(A*A,Lgrad)[:m]` okay. This uses one rfft. Good.
```
def soft_fg(x,beta,n,Lconv,Lgrad,m):
  ... same
  # gradient
  g_half[...] = ...
  Sdot = float(np.dot(g_half,a))
  grad_x = np.empty(n-1)
  if n>1: grad_x = 2.0*n*a[:-1]*(g_half[:-1]-Sdot)
  return f, grad_x, a, cmax
```
Note if p one-hot, g_half row? okay.

Potential issue: `soft_fg` f computed with Lgrad may differ slightly from `soft_f`. In BFGS after line search, we compute `f_full` and use for history. But line search used `soft_f` with Lconv. If f_full > f + tolerance due roundoff, we might reject despite Armijo. Use tolerance 1e-6 relative? Since F~1.5, roundoff maybe 1e-12. Use `f_full <= f* (1+1e-6) + 1e-12`. If not, but cmax improved, accept. Good.

BFGS code with local `fix_last`: x length n-1. Need `m=2*n-1`, `Lconv=next_pow2(m)`, `Lgrad=next_pow2(3*n)`? 3n maybe m+n? To be safe `next_pow2(3*n+1)`.

`bfgs_optimize`:
```
def bfgs_optimize(a0, beta, time_limit, verbose=False, label=""):
    a_init=normalize_positive(a0)
    n=len(a0)
    if n<=1: return a_init.copy(), 2.0*quick? for n=1 score=2
    # add epsilon to all to ensure reference not tiny
    a_start=a_init.copy()
    a_start = np.maximum(a_start, 1e-12)
    a_start /= a_start.sum()
    if a_start[-1] <= 1e-15: # reference tiny, add
       a_start[-1]=1e-12; a_start/=a_start.sum()
    x=np.log(a_start[:-1]/a_start[-1])
    x=np.clip(x,-700.0,700.0)
    m=2*n-1; Lconv=next_pow2(m); Lgrad=next_pow2(3*n+1)
    f,g,a,cmax=soft_fg(x,beta,n,Lconv,Lgrad,m)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_cmax=cmax; best_score=2*n*best_cmax
    t0=time.monotonic(); rng=np.random.default_rng(31337)
    S_hist=[]; Y_hist=[]; R_hist=[]; alpha=0.5; no_improve=0; it=0
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
       it+=1
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
          x=0.5*x; #? not valid
       gnorm=float(np.linalg.norm(g))
       if gnorm < 1e-10: break
       # direction
       if len(S_hist)==0:
          d=-g
       else:
          q=g.copy()
          alphas=np.zeros(len(S_hist))
          for i in range(len(S_hist)-1,-1,-1):
             ai=R_hist[i]*float(np.dot(S_hist[i], q))
             alphas[i]=ai
             q=q-ai*Y_hist[i]
          s_last=S_hist[-1]; y_last=Y_hist[-1]
          dy=float(np.dot(y_last,y_last)); dsy=float(np.dot(s_last,y_last))
          if dy <= 1e-14 or dsy <= 0:
             gamma=1.0
          else:
             gamma=dsy/dy
          r=q*gamma
          for i in range(len(S_hist)):
             bi=R_hist[i]*float(np.dot(Y_hist[i], r))
             r=r+(alphas[i]-bi)*S_hist[i]
          d=-r
       gd=float(np.dot(g,d))
       if gd > -1e-12:
          d=-g; gd=-gnorm*gnorm
          if gd > -1e-12: break
       alpha_ls=alpha
       accepted=False; x_trial=None; f_trial=1e300
       for _ in range(35):
          x_trial=x+alpha_ls*d
          if not np.all(np.isfinite(x_trial)):
             alpha_ls*=0.5; continue
          f_trial=soft_f(x_trial,beta,n,Lconv,m)
          if f_trial <= f + 1e-4*alpha_ls*gd + 1e-15:
             accepted=True; break
          alpha_ls*=0.5
          if alpha_ls < 1e-8: break
       if not accepted:
          # fallback: pure gradient with max alpha 0.05
          d=-g; gd=-gnorm*gnorm; alpha_ls=min(alpha,0.05)
          for _ in range(20):
             x_trial=x+alpha_ls*d
             f_trial=soft_f(x_trial,beta,n,Lconv,m)
             if f_trial <= f*(1+1e-7) + 1e-4*alpha_ls*gd:
                accepted=True; break
             alpha_ls*=0.5
             if alpha_ls<1e-9: break
       if not accepted:
          # restart
          noise_scale=0.02 + 0.05*min(no_improve/50,1.0)
          x=x + rng.standard_normal(n-1)*noise_scale
          x=np.clip(x,-700,700)
          f,g,a,cmax=soft_fg(x,beta,n,Lconv,Lgrad,m)
          S_hist=[]; Y_hist=[]; R_hist=[]
          no_improve += 1
          if no_improve > 100: break
          continue
       # full evaluation
       f_full,g_trial,a_trial,cmax_trial=soft_fg(x_trial,beta,n,Lconv,Lgrad,m)
       # accept if line search acceptance or actual improves
       if f_full <= f*(1+1e-6)+1e-12 or cmax_trial < best_cmax - 1e-13:
          s_new=x_trial-x; y_new=g_trial-g
          ys=float(np.dot(s_new,y_new))
          if ys > 1e-10:
             S_hist.append(s_new); Y_hist.append(y_new); R_hist.append(1.0/ys)
             if len(S_hist)>20:
                S_hist.pop(0); Y_hist.pop(0); R_hist.pop(0)
          x=x_trial; f=f_full; g=g_trial; a=a_trial
          # alpha adapt
          if alpha_ls > 0.01:
             alpha = min(1.0, alpha_ls*2.0)
          elif accepted:
             alpha = min(alpha, alpha_ls*2.0)
          if cmax_trial < best_cmax - 1e-13:
             best_cmax=cmax_trial; best_a=a_trial.copy(); best_score=2*n*best_cmax; no_improve=0
             if verbose and it % 20 == 0: print(f"  {label} bfgs iter {it}: score={best_score:.8f}")
          else:
             no_improve += 1
          if no_improve > 40:
             # small restart
             x=x + rng.standard_normal(n-1)*0.01
             x=np.clip(x,-700,700)
             f,g,a,cmax=soft_fg(x,beta,n,Lconv,Lgrad,m)
             S_hist=[]; Y_hist=[]; R_hist=[]
             no_improve=10
       else:
          no_improve += 1
          if no_improve > 100: break
    return best_a, best_score
```

```
def add_candidate(pool, a, score):
    if a is None or not np.isfinite(score): return
    a=np.asarray(a,float).copy()
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: return
    a/=s
    # avoid duplicates by length and first 8 values hash? simple keep max 30

    if len(pool)>30:
       pool.sort(key=lambda x: x[0]) # lambda not allowed!
```

Let's write `generate_starters` code without nested functions and no lambda:
```
def generate_starters(n, parent=None, limit=40):
    out=[]
    n=int(n)
    # uniform
    a=np.ones(n,float); out.append(a)
    idx=np.arange(1, n+1, dtype=float)
    ridx=n - idx + 1.0
    x=np.arange(n, dtype=float)/n
    # left power
    for p in (0.0, 0.2, 0.4, 0.45, 0.5, 0.6):
        if p == 0.0:
            a=np.ones(n,float)
        else:
            a=idx**(-p)
        s=a.sum()
        if s>0: out.append(a/s)
    # additive mix
    for p in (0.4, 0.45, 0.5):
        left=idx**(-p)
        for q in (0.0, 0.2, 0.4, 0.5):
            right=np.ones(n,float) if q==0 else ridx**(-q)
            for gamma in (0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0):
                a=left + gamma*right
                s=a.sum()
                if s>0 and np.isfinite(s): out.append(a/s)
    # product
    for p in (0.2,0.4,0.5):
        left=idx**(-p)
        for q in (0.0,0.2,0.4):
            right=np.ones(n,float) if q==0 else ridx**(-q)
            a=left*right
            s=a.sum(); if s>0: out.append(a/s)
    # gaussian
    for mu in (0.3,0.5,0.7):
        for sig in (0.1,0.2):
            a=np.exp(-((x-mu)/sig)**2)
            s=a.sum(); if s>0: out.append(a/s)
    if parent is not None:
        a=interpolate_array(parent, n)
        out.append(a)
    # optionally random? Use deterministic no rng. Maybe add a few random smooth via sin:
    for k in (1,2,3):
        a=np.exp(0.5*np.sin(2*np.pi*k*x + 0.5)) * np.exp(-np.pi*x)
        s=a.sum(); if s>0: out.append(a/s)
    if len(out)>limit: out=out[:limit]
    return out
```
Important: If p=0 uniform duplicate many times, okay. We can avoid duplicate but not necessary. Limit may cut some later profiles if many duplicates. The left power loop p=0 adds uniform; additive gamma=0 duplicates left powers. Could waste initial evals. But quick_score cheap. However if limit=45, many duplicates at beginning may exclude interesting later profiles. Better avoid duplicates or order interesting first. Let's reorganize: Add uniform once, inverse sqrt left, additive mixes, product, gauss, parent. Avoid p=0 in left except uniform. For additive, gamma=0 duplicates left; skip if gamma=0? But left powers already. We'll include gamma>0 only. For q=0 right uniform, gamma>0 adds linearly; okay.

```
def safe_normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if a.size==0: return None
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    if s>0 and np.isfinite(s):
       return a/s
    return None
```

```
def trim_ends(a, score, max_rounds=100):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).copy()
    score=float(score)
    for _ in range(max_rounds):
        best_a=a; best_sc=score; improved=False
        if len(a) >= 8:
           b=a[:-1]; bn=safe_normalize(b)
           if bn is not None:
              sc,_,_=quick_score(bn)
              if sc + 1e-13 < best_sc:
                 best_a=bn.copy(); best_sc=sc; improved=True
        if len(a) >= 8:
           b=a[1:]; ...
        if not improved: break
        a=best_a; score=best_sc
    # try collapse small values
    b=a[a > 1e-10]
    if b.size < a.size and b.size >= 8:
       bn=safe_normalize(b)
       if bn is not None:
          sc,_,_=quick_score(bn)
          if sc + 1e-13 < score:
             a=bn; score=sc
    # repeated trim
    ... maybe one more
    return a, score
```

```
def finalize_sequence(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).copy()
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.ones(64)/64
    # If sum too small? scale up to 1 (score invariant but evaluate rejects if sum<0.01). Normalize to 1.
    a = a / s
    return a
```

```python
"""
Optimize the coefficients of a non-negative step function to minimize

    2*n*max(conv(a, a)) / (sum(a))**2

using a combination of:

1. Analytic non-linear initial profiles and interpolation from the provided
   parent construction.
2. A fast active-constraint Frank-Wolfe/LP oracle that alternately solves
   a small linear program based on the currently active convolution peaks.
3. A custom L-BFGS refinement on the softmax/convolution objective with a
   smoothed maximum (log-sum-exp) of the auto-convolution peaks.
4. End trimming and final exact evaluation of a small pool of candidates.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    import scipy.optimize as _scipy_opt
    import scipy.linalg as _scipy_linalg
    HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SCIPY = False

LP_MAX_N = 2048


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def safe_normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
        return a / s
    return None


def normalize_positive(a):
    a = safe_normalize(a)
    if a is None:
        n = max(1, int(np.asarray(a).size))
        return np.ones(n, dtype=float) / n
    return a


def fast_convolve(a, L=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    n = a.size
    size = 2 * n - 1
    if L is None:
        L = next_pow2(size)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    return np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)[:size]


def fast_convolve_cross(a, b, L=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=float)
    n = a.size
    size = 2 * n - 1
    if L is None:
        L = next_pow2(size)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    B = np.fft.rfft(b, L)
    return np.fft.irfft(A * B, L)[:size]


def quick_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf"), 0.0, None
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return float("inf"), 0.0, None
    aa = a / s
    c = fast_convolve(aa)
    if c.size == 0:
        m = 0.0
    else:
        m = float(np.max(c))
    return float(2.0 * aa.size * m), m, aa


def interpolate_array(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    n = int(n)
    if a.size == n:
        return a.copy()
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    b = np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(b))
    if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
        return b / s
    return np.ones(n, dtype=float) / n


def build_conv_matrix(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    n = a.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    first_col = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
    first_col[:n] = a
    first_row = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    first_row[0] = a[0]
    return _scipy_linalg.toeplitz(first_col, first_row)


def min_cross_lp(a, active=None, perturb=None):
    """
    Solve:
        min C
        s.t. (a*g)_k <= C for k in active (or all k)
             sum(g) = 1, g >= 0, C >= 0
    """
    if not HAVE_SCIPY:
        return None, None

    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    n = a.size
    mfull = 2 * n - 1

    if active is None:
        F = build_conv_matrix(a)
        A_ub = np.empty((mfull, n + 1), dtype=float)
        A_ub[:, :n] = F
        A_ub[:, n] = -1.0
        b_ub = np.zeros(mfull, dtype=float)
    else:
        active = np.asarray(active, dtype=int)
        K = active.size
        if K == 0:
            return None, None

        rows = np.zeros((K, n), dtype=float)
        for ii in range(K):
            k = int(active[ii])
            js = max(0, k - n + 1)
            je = min(n - 1, k)
            if je >= js:
                idx = np.arange(js, je + 1)
                rows[ii, idx] = a[k - idx]

        A_ub = np.empty((K, n + 1), dtype=float)
        A_ub[:, :n] = rows
        A_ub[:, n] = -1.0
        b_ub = np.zeros(K, dtype=float)

    A_eq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=float)
    A_eq[0, :n] = 1.0
    b_eq = np.array([1.0], dtype=float)

    c_obj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=float)
    c_obj[n] = 1.0

    if perturb is not None:
        perturb = np.asarray(perturb, dtype=float)
        if perturb.shape == (n,):
            c_obj[:n] -= 1e-7 * perturb

    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * (n + 1)

    try:
        res = _scipy_opt.linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=A_ub,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            A_eq=A_eq,
            b_eq=b_eq,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True},
        )
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = _scipy_opt.linprog(
                c_obj,
                A_ub=A_ub,
                b_ub=b_ub,
                A_eq=A_eq,
                b_eq=b_eq,
                bounds=bounds,
            )
        except Exception:
            return None, None

    if res.status != 0 or not hasattr(res, "x"):
        return None, None

    g = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=float)
    C = float(res.x[n])
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(g))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None, None
    g = g / s
    return g, C


def lp_polish(a0, time_limit, verbose=False, label=""):
    a = normalize_positive(a0)
    n = a.size
    c = fast_convolve(a)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_M = M
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * M)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(314159265)
    full_allowed = n <= 1024

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        it += 1
        if it > 8000:
            break

        c = fast_convolve(a)
        M = float(np.max(c))

        if no_improve > 8 and full_allowed:
            active = None
        else:
            K = min(120, max(60, n // 8))
            if no_improve > 8:
                K = min(200, 2 * n - 1)
            if K >= c.size:
                active = np.arange(c.size)
            else:
                active = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]

        perturb = None
        if no_improve > 5:
            perturb = rng.standard_normal(n)

        g, C_lp = min_cross_lp(a, active=active, perturb=perturb)
        if g is None:
            break

        if float(np.max(np.abs(g - a))) < 1e-13:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 6:
                break
            continue

        Ccross = float(np.max(fast_convolve_cross(a, g)))
        D = float(np.max(fast_convolve(g)))

        vals = []
        seen = set()
        base = [0.001, 0.005, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0]

        if Ccross < M:
            den = M - 2.0 * Ccross + D
            if den > 1e-14:
                tstar = (M - Ccross) / den
                for fac in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.25, 1.5):
                    t = tstar * fac
                    if 0.0 < t < 1.0:
                        base.append(float(t))

        for t in base:
            key = round(float(t), 6)
            if key not in seen:
                seen.add(key)
                vals.append(float(t))

        best_at = None
        best_t = None
        best_M_iter = M

        for t in vals:
            at = (1.0 - t) * a + t * g
            at = np.maximum(at, 0.0)
            st = float(np.sum(at))
            if st <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(st):
                continue
            at = at / st
            obj = float(np.max(fast_convolve(at)))
            if obj < best_M_iter - 1e-14:
                best_M_iter = obj
                best_at = at
                best_t = t

        if best_at is None:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 14:
                break
            continue

        a = best_at
        M = best_M_iter
        score = float(2.0 * n * M)

        if score < best_score - 1e-11:
            best_score = score
            best_a = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            if verbose and it % 5 == 0:
                print(f"  {label} lp iter {it}: score={best_score:.8f}, t={best_t:.3f}, Ccross={Ccross:.8e}")
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 45:
            break

    return best_a, best_score


def soft_f(x, beta, n, Lconv, m):
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e300

    xext = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    xext[:-1] = x
    xext[-1] = 0.0

    mx = float(np.max(xext))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return 1e300

    e = np.exp(xext - mx)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=float) / n
    else:
        a = e / s

    A = np.fft.rfft(a, Lconv)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, Lconv)[:m]
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return 1e300

    shifted = beta * (c - cmax)
    S = float(np.exp(shifted).sum())
    if S <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(S):
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        p = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
        p[idx] = 1.0
        S = 1.0
    else:
        lse = cmax + np.log(S) / beta
        f = n * lse
        if not np.isfinite(f):
            return 1e300
        return f

    lse = cmax + np.log(S) / beta
    f = n * lse
    if not np.isfinite(f):
        return 1e300
    return f


def soft_fg(x, beta, n, Lconv, Lgrad, m):
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e300, np.zeros(max(0, n - 1)), np.ones(n) / n, 0.0

    xext = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    xext[:-1] = x
    xext[-1] = 0.0

    mx = float(np.max(xext))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return 1e300, np.zeros(max(0, n - 1)), np.ones(n) / n, 0.0

    e = np.exp(xext - mx)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=float) / n
    else:
        a = e / s

    A = np.fft.rfft(a, Lgrad)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, Lgrad)[:m]
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return 1e300, np.zeros(max(0, n - 1)), a, 0.0

    shifted = beta * (c - cmax)
    w = np.exp(shifted)
    S = float(np.sum(w))
    if S <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(S):
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        p = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
        p[idx] = 1.0
        S = 1.0
    else:
        p = w / S

    lse = cmax + np.log(S) / beta
    f = n * lse

    P = np.zeros(Lgrad, dtype=float)
    P[:m] = p
    PH = np.fft.rfft(P)
    g_half = np.fft.irfft(PH * np.conj(A), Lgrad)[:n]
    g_half = np.asarray(g_half, dtype=float)

    Sdot = float(np.dot(g_half, a))
    grad_x = np.zeros(max(0, n - 1), dtype=float)
    if n > 1:
        grad_x = 2.0 * n * a[:-1] * (g_half[:-1] - Sdot)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_x)):
        grad_x = np.zeros(max(0, n - 1), dtype=float)

    return f, grad_x, a, cmax


def bfgs_optimize(a0, beta, time_limit, verbose=False, label=""):
    a0 = np.asarray(a0, dtype=float)
    n = a0.size
    if n <= 1:
        a = normalize_positive(a0)
        scl, _, _ = quick_score(a)
        return a, scl

    a_start = a0.copy()
    a_start = np.maximum(a_start, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a_start))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        a_start = np.ones(n, dtype=float) / n
    else:
        a_start = a_start / s

    a_start = np.maximum(a_start, 1e-12)
    if a_start[-1] <= 1e-15:
        a_start[-1] = 1e-12
        a_start = a_start / a_start.sum()

    x = np.log(a_start[:-1] / a_start[-1])
    x = np.clip(x, -700.0, 700.0)

    m = 2 * n - 1
    Lconv = next_pow2(m)
    Lgrad = next_pow2(3 * n + 1)

    f, g, a, cmax = soft_fg(x, beta, n, Lconv, Lgrad, m)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_cmax = cmax
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * best_cmax)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(271828182)
    S_hist = []
    Y_hist = []
    R_hist = []
    alpha = 0.5
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        it += 1

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)) or not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            x = x * 0.5
            x = np.clip(x, -700.0, 700.0)
            f, g, a, cmax = soft_fg(x, beta, n, Lconv, Lgrad, m)
            S_hist = []
            Y_hist = []
            R_hist = []
            continue

        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        if gnorm < 1e-10:
            break

        if len(S_hist) == 0:
            d = -g
        else:
            q = g.copy()
            alphas = np.zeros(len(S_hist), dtype=float)
            for i in range(len(S_hist) - 1, -1, -1):
                ai = R_hist[i] * float(np.dot(S_hist[i], q))
                alphas[i] = ai
                q = q - ai * Y_hist[i]

            s_last = S_hist[-1]
            y_last = Y_hist[-1]
            yy = float(np.dot(y_last, y_last))
            yyss = float(np.dot(s_last, y_last))
            if yy <= 1e-14 or yyss <= 0.0:
                gamma = 1.0
            else:
                gamma = yyss / yy

            r = q * gamma
            for i in range(len(S_hist)):
                bi = R_hist[i] * float(np.dot(Y_hist[i], r))
                r = r + (alphas[i] - bi) * S_hist[i]

            d = -r

        gd = float(np.dot(g, d))
        if gd > -1e-12:
            d = -g
            gd = -gnorm * gnorm
            if gd > -1e-12:
                break

        dnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
        if dnorm > 1e6:
            d = d * (1e6 / dnorm)
            gd = gd * (1e6 / dnorm)

        alpha_ls = alpha
        accepted = False
        x_trial = None
        f_trial = 1e300

        for _ in range(35):
            x_trial = x + alpha_ls * d
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(x_trial)):
                alpha_ls *= 0.5
                continue

            f_trial = soft_f(x_trial, beta, n, Lconv, m)
            if not np.isfinite(f_trial):
                alpha_ls *= 0.5
                continue

            if f_trial <= f + 1e-4 * alpha_ls * gd + 1e-15:
                accepted = True
                break

            alpha_ls *= 0.5
            if alpha_ls < 1e-8:
                break

        if not accepted:
            d = -g
            gd = -gnorm * gnorm
            alpha_ls = min(alpha, 0.05)
            for _ in range(20):
                x_trial = x + alpha_ls * d
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(x_trial)):
                    alpha_ls *= 0.5
                    continue
                f_trial = soft_f(x_trial, beta, n, Lconv, m)
                if np.isfinite(f_trial) and f_trial <= f * (1.0 + 1e-7) + 1e-4 * alpha_ls * gd:
                    accepted = True
                    break
                alpha_ls *= 0.5
                if alpha_ls < 1e-9:
                    break

        if not accepted:
            noise_scale = 0.02 + 0.05 * min(float(no_improve) / 50.0, 1.0)
            x = x + rng.standard_normal(n - 1) * noise_scale
            x = np.clip(x, -700.0, 700.0)
            f, g, a, cmax = soft_fg(x, beta, n, Lconv, Lgrad, m)
            S_hist = []
            Y_hist = []
            R_hist = []
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 90:
                break
            continue

        f_full, g_trial, a_trial, cmax_trial = soft_fg(x_trial, beta, n, Lconv, Lgrad, m)

        if (np.isfinite(f_full) and f_full <= f * (1.0 + 1e-6) + 1e-12) or (
            np.isfinite(cmax_trial) and cmax_trial < best_cmax - 1e-13
        ):
            s_new = x_trial - x
            y_new = g_trial - g
            ys = float(np.dot(s_new, y_new))
            if ys > 1e-10:
                S_hist.append(s_new)
                Y_hist.append(y_new)
                R_hist.append(1.0 / ys)
                if len(S_hist) > 20:
                    S_hist.pop(0)
                    Y_hist.pop(0)
                    R_hist.pop(0)

            x = x_trial
            f = f_full
            g = g_trial
            a = a_trial

            if alpha_ls > 0.01:
                alpha = min(1.0, alpha_ls * 2.0)
            else:
                alpha = min(alpha, alpha_ls * 2.0)

            if cmax_trial < best_cmax - 1e-13:
                best_cmax = float(cmax_trial)
                best_a = a_trial.copy()
                best_score = float(2.0 * n * best_cmax)
                no_improve = 0
                if verbose and it % 25 == 0:
                    print(f"  {label} bfgs iter {it}: score={best_score:.8f}, beta={beta:.0f}")
            else:
                no_improve += 1

            if no_improve > 45:
                x = x + rng.standard_normal(n - 1) * 0.01
                x = np.clip(x, -700.0, 700.0)
                f, g, a, cmax = soft_fg(x, beta, n, Lconv, Lgrad, m)
                S_hist = []
                Y_hist = []
                R_hist = []
                no_improve = 10
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 90:
                break

    return best_a, best_score


def generate_starters(n, parent=None, limit=40):
    out = []
    n = int(n)

    if parent is not None:
        b = interpolate_array(parent, n)
        out.append(b)

    b = safe_normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=float))
    if b is not None:
        out.append(b)

    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=float)
    ridx = n - idx + 1.0
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=float) / n

    for p in (0.3, 0.45, 0.5, 0.6, 0.7):
        a = idx ** (-p)
        b = safe_normalize(a)
        if b is not None:
            out.append(b)

    for p in (0.45, 0.5, 0.6):
        left = idx ** (-p)
        for q in (0.0, 0.2, 0.4, 0.5):
            right = np.ones(n, dtype=float) if q == 0.0 else ridx ** (-q)
            for gamma in (0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
                a = left + gamma * right
                b = safe_normalize(a)
                if b is not None:
                    out.append(b)

    for p in (0.3, 0.5):
        left = idx ** (-p)
        for q in (0.2, 0.4, 0.6):
            right = ridx ** (-q)
            a = left * right
            b = safe_normalize(a)
            if b is not None:
                out.append(b)

    for mu in (0.3, 0.5, 0.7):
        for sig in (0.1, 0.2):
            a = np.exp(-((x - mu) / sig) ** 2) * np.exp(-0.7 * x)
            b = safe_normalize(a)
            if b is not None:
                out.append(b)

    for k in (1, 2, 3):
        a = np.exp(0.5 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + 0.5)) * np.exp(-np.pi * x)
        b = safe_normalize(a)
        if b is not None:
            out.append(b)

    if len(out) > limit:
        out = out[:limit]
    return out


def add_candidate(pool, a, score, max_keep=30):
    if a is None or not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return
    a = a / s
    pool.append((float(score), a))

    if len(pool) > max_keep:
        worst_idx = 0
        worst_score = pool[0][0]
        for i in range(1, len(pool)):
            if pool[i][0] > worst_score:
                worst_score = pool[i][0]
                worst_idx = i
        del pool[worst_idx]


def trim_ends(a, score, max_rounds=100):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).copy()
    score = float(score)

    for _ in range(max_rounds):
        best_a = a
        best_sc = score
        improved = False

        if a.size >= 8:
            b = safe_normalize(a[:-1])
            if b is not None:
                sc, _, _ = quick_score(b)
                if sc + 1e-13 < best_sc:
                    best_a = b
                    best_sc = float(sc)
                    improved = True

        if a.size >= 8:
            b = safe_normalize(a[1:])
            if b is not None:
                sc, _, _ = quick_score(b)
                if sc + 1e-13 < best_sc:
                    best_a = b
                    best_sc = float(sc)
                    improved = True

        if not improved:
            break
        a = best_a
        score = best_sc

    b = a[a > 1e-10]
    if b.size < a.size and b.size >= 8:
        bn = safe_normalize(b)
        if bn is not None:
            sc, _, _ = quick_score(bn)
            if sc + 1e-13 < score:
                a = bn
                score = float(sc)

    for _ in range(20):
        best_a = a
        best_sc = score
        improved = False

        if a.size >= 8:
            b = safe_normalize(a[:-1])
            if b is not None:
                sc, _, _ = quick_score(b)
                if sc + 1e-13 < best_sc:
                    best_a = b
                    best_sc = float(sc)
                    improved = True

        if a.size >= 8:
            b = safe_normalize(a[1:])
            if b is not None:
                sc, _, _ = quick_score(b)
                if sc + 1e-13 < best_sc:
                    best_a = b
                    best_sc = float(sc)
                    improved = True

        if not improved:
            break
        a = best_a
        score = best_sc

    return a, score


def finalize_sequence(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).copy()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(64, dtype=float) / 64
    return a / s


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    deadline = start_time + max(1.0, budget_s - 30.0)

    pool = []
    best_a = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    parent = None
    parent_score = float("inf")
    n_parent = 768

    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=float)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if p.ndim == 1 and p.size > 0:
            s = float(np.sum(p))
            if s > 1e-2:
                parent = p / s
                n_parent = int(parent.size)
                parent_score, _, pnorm = quick_score(parent)
                add_candidate(pool, parent, parent_score)
                best_a = pnorm.copy()
                best_score = float(parent_score)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    raw_ns = [
        n_parent,
        768,
        1024,
        int(n_parent * 1.25),
        int(n_parent * 1.5),
        int(n_parent * 2),
    ]
    n_list = []
    for nn in raw_ns:
        nn = int(nn)
        if 64 <= nn <= 2048:
            dup = False
            for mm in n_list:
                if mm == nn:
                    dup = True
                    break
            if not dup:
                n_list.append(nn)

    init_deadline = time.monotonic() + min(45.0, max(10.0, budget_s * 0.05))
    init_best = {}

    for nn in n_list:
        if time.monotonic() > init_deadline:
            break
        starters = generate_starters(nn, parent, limit=35 if nn >= 1200 else 45)
        local_best_a = None
        local_best_sc = float("inf")

        for a0 in starters:
            if time.monotonic() > init_deadline:
                break
            sc, _, an = quick_score(a0)
            if sc < local_best_sc:
                local_best_sc = float(sc)
                local_best_a = an.copy()

        if local_best_a is not None and np.isfinite(local_best_sc):
            init_best[nn] = (local_best_a.astype(float), local_best_sc)
            add_candidate(pool, local_best_a, local_best_sc)
            if local_best_sc < best_score:
                best_score = local_best_sc
                best_a = local_best_a.copy()

    # LP runs on top active-constraint candidates.
    lp_candidates = []
    if parent is not None and n_parent <= LP_MAX_N:
        lp_candidates.append((parent_score, parent.copy(), n_parent))
    for nn in n_list:
        if nn <= LP_MAX_N and nn in init_best:
            a_c, sc_c = init_best[nn]
            lp_candidates.append((float(sc_c), a_c.copy(), nn))

    top_lp = []
    tmp_lp = list(lp_candidates)
    for _ in range(min(3, len(tmp_lp))):
        best_idx = 0
        best_sc = tmp_lp[0][0]
        for i in range(1, len(tmp_lp)):
            if tmp_lp[i][0] < best_sc:
                best_sc = tmp_lp[i][0]
                best_idx = i
        top_lp.append(tmp_lp.pop(best_idx))

    lp_runs = 0
    for sc_cand, a_cand, nn in top_lp:
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining < 120.0 or lp_runs >= 3:
            break
        if lp_runs > 0 and sc_cand > best_score + 5e-5:
            continue

        a_use = a_cand.copy()
        sc_use = float(sc_cand)

        if best_a is not None and len(best_a) == nn:
            blend = 0.7 * a_use + 0.3 * best_a
            bn = safe_normalize(blend)
            if bn is not None:
                scb, _, bnorm = quick_score(bn)
                if scb < sc_use:
                    a_use = bnorm.copy()
                    sc_use = float(scb)
                    add_candidate(pool, a_use, sc_use)
                    if sc_use < best_score:
                        best_score = sc_use
                        best_a = a_use.copy()

        t_lp = min(450.0, max(60.0, remaining * 0.35))
        a_tmp, sc_tmp = lp_polish(a_use, time_limit=t_lp, verbose=True, label=f"LP_n{nn}")
        add_candidate(pool, a_tmp, sc_tmp)
        if np.isfinite(sc_tmp) and sc_tmp < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc_tmp)
            best_a = a_tmp.copy()
        lp_runs += 1

    # Primary BFGS refinement.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if best_a is not None and len(best_a) <= 2048 and remaining > 120.0:
        t_b = min(300.0, max(60.0, remaining * 0.35))
        beta = 30000.0 if len(best_a) <= 1024 else 15000.0
        a_tmp, sc_tmp = bfgs_optimize(best_a, beta=beta, time_limit=t_b, verbose=True, label="BFGS_best")
        add_candidate(pool, a_tmp, sc_tmp)
        if np.isfinite(sc_tmp) and sc_tmp < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc_tmp)
            best_a = a_tmp.copy()

    # Second LP polish on the current best if feasible.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if best_a is not None and len(best_a) <= LP_MAX_N and remaining > 120.0:
        t_lp2 = min(350.0, max(60.0, remaining * 0.35))
        a_tmp, sc_tmp = lp_polish(best_a, time_limit=t_lp2, verbose=True, label="LP_polish_best")
        add_candidate(pool, a_tmp, sc_tmp)
        if np.isfinite(sc_tmp) and sc_tmp < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc_tmp)
            best_a = a_tmp.copy()

    # High-temperature/high-precision BFGS polish.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if best_a is not None and len(best_a) <= 2048 and remaining > 80.0:
        t_b2 = min(200.0, max(40.0, remaining * 0.3))
        beta2 = 120000.0 if len(best_a) <= 1024 else 60000.0
        a_tmp, sc_tmp = bfgs_optimize(best_a, beta=beta2, time_limit=t_b2, verbose=True, label="BFGS_polish")
        add_candidate(pool, a_tmp, sc_tmp)
        if np.isfinite(sc_tmp) and sc_tmp < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc_tmp)
            best_a = a_tmp.copy()

    # Secondary BFGS runs from diverse initial basins.
    sec_candidates = []
    for nn, (a_c, sc_c) in init_best.items():
        sec_candidates.append((float(sc_c), a_c.copy(), nn))

    top_sec = []
    tmp_sec = list(sec_candidates)
    for _ in range(min(3, len(tmp_sec))):
        best_idx = 0
        best_sc = tmp_sec[0][0]
        for i in range(1, len(tmp_sec)):
            if tmp_sec[i][0] < best_sc:
                best_sc = tmp_sec[i][0]
                best_idx = i
        top_sec.append(tmp_sec.pop(best_idx))

    for sc_cand, a_cand, nn in top_sec:
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining < 90.0:
            break
        if a_cand is None or len(a_cand) > 2048:
            continue
        if best_a is not None and len(a_cand) == len(best_a) and sc_cand > best_score + 2e-5:
            continue
        if sc_cand > best_score + 1e-4:
            continue

        t_sec = min(150.0, max(60.0, remaining * 0.3))
        beta_sec = 60000.0 if nn <= 1024 else 30000.0
        a_tmp, sc_tmp = bfgs_optimize(a_cand, beta=beta_sec, time_limit=t_sec, verbose=True, label=f"BFGS_sec_n{nn}")
        add_candidate(pool, a_tmp, sc_tmp)
        if np.isfinite(sc_tmp) and sc_tmp < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc_tmp)
            best_a = a_tmp.copy()

    # Trim ends of current best; this can be surprisingly helpful after LP/BFGS.
    if best_a is not None:
        trimmed, trimmed_sc = trim_ends(best_a, best_score)
        add_candidate(pool, trimmed, trimmed_sc)
        if np.isfinite(trimmed_sc) and trimmed_sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(trimmed_sc)
            best_a = trimmed

    # Final exact evaluation over a small candidate pool.
    final_a = None
    final_sc = float("inf")

    for _, a_cand in pool:
        a_fin = finalize_sequence(a_cand)
        try:
            sc_exact = float(evaluate_sequence(a_fin.tolist()))
        except Exception:
            sc_exact = float("inf")
        if np.isfinite(sc_exact) and sc_exact < final_sc:
            final_sc = sc_exact
            final_a = a_fin.copy()

    if final_a is None:
        if best_a is not None:
            final_a = finalize_sequence(best_a)
        else:
            final_a = np.ones(64, dtype=float) / 64

    try:
        sc_check = float(evaluate_sequence(final_a.tolist()))
    except Exception:
        sc_check = float("inf")

    if not np.isfinite(sc_check):
        final_a = np.ones(64, dtype=float) / 64
        try:
            sc_check = float(evaluate_sequence(final_a.tolist()))
        except Exception:
            sc_check = float("inf")

    if verbose := True:
        print(f"FINAL quick/exact candidate score: {sc_check:.8f}")

    return [float(x) for x in finalize_sequence(final_a)]
```
